## Selected delivery research and actual Chrome access — 2026-10-08

The two parallel research reports are saved and the proposed integrated design
is concrete: [selected APK operations](analysis/2026-10-08-apk-selected-features-research.md),
[incident pipeline](analysis/2026-10-08-cctv-incident-research.md), and
[service/incident design](superpowers/specs/2026-10-08-cctv-service-completion-design.md).
The proposed service-owned source broker separates persistent service grants
from fresh individual web publication grants; it requires a new architecture
review because the current session-owned two-base profile cannot support two
independent AI originals plus two independent web originals. Existing limits
are not silently duplicated/relaxed. MP4 evidence also requires an actual media
validation/storage/Range extension beyond JPEG/PNG assets. None of these new
brokers, model adapters, incident workflows or selected-feature executors was
implemented or runtime accepted while researching and writing the design.

The user selected live12/measurement/lifecycle, recorder playback/export,
capture/local recording/layouts, controls/settings, account/sharing and alarm/files,
and emphasized CCTV abnormal-behavior detection/incidents as the main feature.
Read [incident research](analysis/2026-10-08-cctv-incident-research.md) before
implementing the new ingestion/detection/evidence/incident subsystem. It reuses
the original YOLOX/ByteTrack/temporal-rule scope and distinguishes proposed
models/contracts from measured behavior. Current viewer-scoped streams and
JPEG/PNG assets do not provide a background analysis/archive port by themselves.

[Actual Chrome access test](analysis/2026-10-08-chrome-access-test.md): GPT Chrome
control connected and opened localhost3543 login, but the actual page showed
ERR_CONNECTION_REFUSED. Independent bounded HTTPS3543/HTTP3000 requests also
refused. Error screenshot saved; no certificate bypass, dummy server, product
change or runtime startup occurred. This is no application/CCTV acceptance.

### Post-reboot continuation — 2026-10-04 (Claude Code controller)

Resumed per the [reboot handoff](analysis/2026-10-04-reboot-handoff.md) in the
`codex/superlive-web-service` worktree (HEAD `211d1484…`, media work still
uncommitted). The Superpowers plugin (6.4.1, `claude-plugins-official`) was
installed at the user's request; this session followed its subagent-driven
development and review process from the plugin's skill files. The author-frozen
hashes of `native_source.py` and `live.py` matched, and Root reran the focused
media selection: 42 passed, 0 skipped.

Findings and fixes are recorded in the
[liveness review](analysis/2026-10-04-local-media-liveness-review.md):

- Worker wiring from handoff section 3 was implemented and reviewed (fast
  permit, off-loop SQL with identity re-checks).
- Two independent reviews found that the 200 ms emission permit made playback
  liveness depend on decoded-frame cadence: one ~220 ms gap, a ≤5 fps channel,
  or ~20 ms SQL latency permanently closed the peer in inert reproductions.
- Root measured the media admission's `NullPool` SQL path on this machine at
  p50 25 ms / p95 ~125 ms / max 182 ms per transaction (pooled: 0.4–2.3 ms).
  This is consistent with, but does not prove the cause of, the earlier failed
  runs' native queue saturation.
- Corrections (rulings R106–R110): bounded pooled media admission engine; a
  third owned authority-heartbeat thread renewing the permit every 50 ms from
  fresh SQL; non-terminal staleness in the WebRTC transport (drop while stale,
  fence after 2 s, revocation/stop/expiry still terminal); fewer serialized SQL
  checks; proof/retirement SQL off the event loop; honest quarantine when an
  aiortc original close raises.
- Executed in this session: owned real-SQL admission suite with pooling
  15 passed / 0 skipped with owned-database cleanup and source preservation;
  Root's final combined unit run 111 passed with Ruff, Ruff format and strict
  mypy clean. Re-reviews addressed all 17 original findings and the follow-up
  round (2000 ms read deadline restored, quarantine semantics, `max()`
  permit); final verdict for that delta: open blocking findings C0/I0.

**Actual acceptance results (real recorders, real Chrome):**

- Accepted: trusted HTTPS; OWNER login; device checks 8 and 4 channels; real

- **Not accepted: continuous playback.** Every session stopped delivering
  video 29–30 s after the native session opened. Diagnosis ruled out SQL
  authority, ICE consent, network loss and CPU starvation. The decompiled
  official client sends an 8-byte N9000 heartbeat every 6 s (`fu3.N1` →
  `cw3.v5()`), while our native live loop never sends one, so the recorder ends
  the stream ~30 s after open. Ruling R111 adds the keepalive in
  `windows_socket_worker.serve_live`; it requires an independent native review
  and a new native receipt before the next device run.

**Multi-view Phase 0 measurement — 2026-10-05 (measurement only, no source
change).** A run on the real recorders with the unchanged approval c5
([liveness review §6c](analysis/2026-10-04-local-media-liveness-review.md)) measured
the per-stream cost:

- **SQL and WAL.** One main stream costs about 665 SQL transactions/s and
  200 KB/s of WAL. Two cost 1,263 tx/s and 393 KB/s.
- **CPU.** The runtime Python process used 54% of a core for one stream and
  134% for two, and Chrome began dropping frames.
- **Cause.** The authority hot path runs a pooled, `FOR SHARE`-locking SQL
  check (plus a pre-ping) about 33 times per frame. Final-review item I3 is
  therefore the first scaling blocker; caps alone cannot reach twelve streams.
- **No-video channel, corrected.** Store 1 channel 1 alone ends at the 20 s
  start deadline with HTTP 504. The earlier 429s came from concurrent starts on
  the wall.
- **APK comparison.** The official app reads no recorder connection or stream
  limits locally. It opens sub streams with command 1285, whose reply carries
  only an advisory code, and routes media by channel GUID only.

The detailed stage-1 plan, **proposed and awaiting the user's approval**, is
[`2026-10-05-multiview-stage1-plan.md`](superpowers/plans/2026-10-05-multiview-stage1-plan.md):

- a 100 ms per-lease authority cache over a non-locking SQL check;
- sub-stream opens;
- 16-stream caps with bounded waits;
- a 10 s first-frame bound with a distinct no-signal code;
- a wall policy of sub streams in the grid and the main stream when enlarged;
- a sizing gate on the real recorders.

Nothing in that plan was implemented when this entry was written.

### Trusted Windows development TLS

The user explicitly requested a durable fix for the test certificate warning.
The previous fixture generated an untrusted test-ca/server chain with about one
hour of validity for every owned run. Root added
`scripts/dev/setup-local-https.ps1`, using official mkcert1.4.4 and the SHA256
published in Microsoft's winget manifest. The dedicated CA/key/server files
remain outside Git under a Windows ACL limited to the current user and SYSTEM.
Only that CA certificate was added to CurrentUser/Root; no machine-wide trust
store or browser validation flag was changed. The localhost leaf also covers
127.0.0.1 and ::1, expires2029-01-03 UTC, and passes Windows chain verification.
Root directly opened fresh Chrome tabs at HTTPS localhost and127.0.0.1 port8765;
both rendered the certificate-check page without a warning. The latter address
had no previous manual warning exception. A screenshot records the check.
This is actual TLS/browser acceptance; the full service fixture is being updated
to consume these same persistent files through an explicit test-only setting.
Its internal bridge mTLS certificates retain their separate ownership.

The optional TLS/publication Fix1 passed independent C0/I0/M0 review and Root
verification of6 sources plus57 new artifacts (receipt71dc188e03106178f31f836828bdb2e57ea16a6df48fecad855241f7c43105ee).
Fresh owned startup then exposed a compatibility error before browser readiness:
the helper required the optional BasicConstraints extension on an end-entity
leaf. Official mkcert omits it; its CA has BasicConstraints and both signatures,
SANs and Windows/Chrome trust remain valid. The scoped correction accepts absent
leaf BasicConstraints while continuing to reject a CA leaf and require a valid
CA. This follows [RFC5280 section4.2.1.9](https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc5280#section-4.2.1.9).
Failed run75dacc180db549edbc5aa86dca24bb2e fully settled with exit1, removed
owned state/database and zero known-private output hits; it is not service
acceptance. The original author is adding the missing regression before retry.

Sources: [mkcert official documentation](https://github.com/FiloSottile/mkcert),
[pinned Windows release checksum](https://github.com/microsoft/winget-pkgs/blob/master/manifests/f/FiloSottile/mkcert/1.4.4/FiloSottile.mkcert.installer.yaml),
[Chrome locally managed certificate support](https://chromium.googlesource.com/chromium/src/+/main/net/data/ssl/chrome_root_store/faq.md).

### Windows implementation decision and executed limits

Android APK libraries cannot be loaded as Windows DLLs. Two concrete local routes
are now available: the already executed Windows-owned Android diagnostic runtime,
and the recovered Windows AMD64 raw transport package. The selected Windows
provider uses precise ctypes bindings inside one separately owned helper process.
The Python [ctypes documentation](https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/ctypes.html)
supports calling DLL functions and requires retaining callback objects. Microsoft's
[LoadLibraryEx documentation](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/api/libloaderapi/nf-libloaderapi-loadlibraryexw)
defines the restricted dependency-search flags used in the implementation brief.
These primary references verify the interop method; they do not establish vendor
compatibility or real device success.

The current CPython process already maps Microsoft vcruntime140.dll14.42.34438.0,
which differs from the exact SDK14.28 package payload. Root read the existing
mapped module identity and verified all26 vendor-imported symbols through that
existing module handle, without loading/calling vendor code. A single immutable
runtime-override receipt permits this exact Microsoft runtime for development
probes; no vendor DLL or generic same-name replacement is allowed. Microsoft's
[v14 compatibility guidance](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/cpp/porting/binary-compat-2015-2017?view=msvc-170)
supports newer runtimes for earlier v14 components but requires qualification
against the newest tools used. The Python MSC1943 banner versus runtime14.42
does not establish that final production qualification; actual transport calls
and deployment runtime validation remain required. DLL attachment passed using
that exact runtime. Package files have protected
explicit current-user/SYSTEM ACLs; installed Python runtime ACLs were not changed.

Actual product-worker tracing found an additional existing CPython
VCRUNTIME140_1.dll14.42.34438.0 (49776 bytes) before the TVT load. Its name/path/hash
differs from the packaged runtime and was correctly rejected. A new immutable
two-runtime receipt preserves the original evidence and pins both Microsoft
files;26 plus1 required vendor imports resolve on those existing modules. Only
these exact runtime identities may be admitted. All original fourteen package
bytes remain required and TVT/vendor substitutions remain rejected. No installed
runtime or global ACL was changed. Development-only and unproved production
toolset qualification remain explicit.

Native callbacks must only copy bounded bytes into a private queue. The physical
observer and DLLs remain alive until helper-process termination because vendor
callback quiescence is unproved. The parent owns the original child handle and

uncertain cleanup. Credentials are admitted privately once and login responses

greeting64, positive Send, code0 and extracted key are separately recorded stages;
none alone grants device/channel commands or claims a usable frame.

## Source baseline and purpose

The intended service provides the complete functional behavior of SuperLive Plus
as a React/Next.js and Tailwind frontend with Python/FastAPI backend, protected
workers and a managed gateway where the APK uses device-native communication.
The supplied APK analysis is the implementation authority. A separate vendor SDK
or API document is not a prerequisite for continuing development.

This refreshed review uses branch `codex/superlive-web-service`, published baseline
`756946ca73cff31d7ebb23141873088b470a96b4`, and the inspected uncommitted changes
in the existing `superlive-web-service` worktree. The earlier `4df15be` assessment
and subsequent failed runs remain dated historical evidence. The APK baseline is
SuperLive Plus **1.18.1**, SHA-256
`f57ff98226fcc7a0ec3587b077d5538facb57cc5b713a0938d1ee02b0f72f281`.
The [implementation plan](superpowers/plans/2026-09-27-superlive-plus-web-parity-implementation-plan.md)
contains 26 work packages, W00–W25, and a frozen seed of 77 comparison cases /
199 candidate operation boundaries. These are inventories, not completed features.

Unpublished work includes private SID acquisition5, directory browser runtime5,
targeted device-access source completion, reviewed daemon-helper9, and root
status documentation. `apps/web/next-env.d.ts` is an unowned generated change and
is excluded from reviewed publication. No unfinished file is approved by presence.

## Architecture actually present

```mermaid
flowchart LR
    FE[React / Next.js / Tailwind] --> API[FastAPI]
    API --> PG[PostgreSQL: sessions, tenant authority, jobs, TVT metadata]
    API --> RPC[mTLS account RPC client]
    RPC --> AW[Protected account worker]
    AW --> HTTP[APK-derived account HTTPS adapter]
    Jobs[Durable dispatcher and worker] --> PG
    Jobs --> Broker[Valkey / Celery]
    Jobs --> Assets[Private asset primitives]
    Gateway[Android JNI helper: host scaffold] -. runtime integration pending .-> Device[Device / media native path]
```

The published API has startup, six TVT account routes and eight account-flow
routes. Six readonly directory routes and their opted-in React views are published; the full device/media/cloud API surface remains pending. Account worker
configuration and token-vault access belong to the
separate worker; API construction creates its RPC client. Database-issued authority
and current membership checks prevent a caller-supplied identity or UUID from
authorizing another user's credentials. The Android helper and frame-parser
scaffolding do not establish working device or video sessions.

| W18 VAS/payment | Operation primitives / APK analysis | Catalog/eligibility/order/subscription/renewal/auto-renew/payment-return flows, authoritative order reconciliation and no double charge |
| W19 Tyco | Separate Tyco models / APK analysis | Separate login/registration, panel/device/partition/output controls, events/troubles/users/time/settings and notifications; retain independent credentials/domain |
| W20 files | Private JPEG/PNG image metadata, crypto, upload/download/image/job primitives; this is not general video-file support | User-facing image/video collection/search/view/play/download/share/delete, general media formats and retention/storage-error behavior; T05A provider acceptance remains open |
| W21 personalization | Locale/timezone preferences from W03 | Auto-connect, orientation/OSD/PTZ gesture, snapshot/record preferences, favorites/start screen/cache/Wi-Fi/domain and effect verification |
| W22 help/web content | Four bundled terms/privacy routes | Help/About/version/experience and brand/debug pages, delivered H5 action semantics, household/resident-manager flows, safe navigation and branch reachability |
| W23 security/operations | Existing tenant/credential/CSRF/mTLS and job recovery controls | Apply/test those boundaries across every new feature; device/media reconnect, expiry, audit/redaction/metrics/alerts and orphan-session reconciliation |
| W24 APK comparison | Static ledger/checker | Full APK reference/web candidate runner, trusted comparison/reporting, same-state success/denial/offline/expiry/unsupported cases and independent acceptance |
| W25 release/deployment | Generic Linux CI and existing deployment primitives | TVT gateway/service deployment, production identity/key/certificate/profile provisioning, dependency readiness, restore/upgrade/rollback, drift monitoring and release decision |

continuation above separately records actual DLL/helper/device runs and source
development tests, with exact limits; no deployment/database migration or APK
comparative acceptance was executed. Evidence was read from current code,
source/review packets and retained actual run receipts. There
is no defensible service-completion percentage or fixed completion date from
these results. Zero matched cases does not mean zero code implemented.

## Priorities and execution order

1. **Finish current handoffs:** review private SID capture and current Windows
   directory browser runtime, then root starts servers and operates Chrome through
   GPT web-control. Keep ordinary synthetic CI and direct/live acceptance separate.
2. **Complete protected security:** scope encrypted SID/key lineage to current
   USER credential, add durable write admission/readback, API/RPC/React for profile,
   phone/email and password, then provider/avatar/QR/removal. Use exact APK source
   contracts; no missing vendor document prerequisite.
3. **Build the core surveillance path:** readonly device/channel directory and
   capability/permission state, then native/gateway session transport, live video
   and multiview. Recording search/playback/export, Talk and controls follow their
   decoded contracts, with independent local work in parallel.
4. **Repair actual storage/runtime failures in parallel:** parent-read denial,
   daemon-helper publication/POSIX proof, and remaining full14 initiating failures.
   These are needed by media/file features; unchanged failing heavy suites should
   not be repeatedly rerun without a relevant fix.
5. **Complete all remaining domains:** events/push, onboarding, AI, cloud, VAS,
   Tyco, file manager, personalization and remote/help/household pages. Local UI
   work need not wait for a separate vendor document.
6. **Close comparison and deployment:** all reachable applicable cases, role
   isolation/expiry/offline/permission variants, production runtime/restore and
   final independent release review.

## Risks and product questions

- The previous implementation ledger's broad “W03–W25 pending” row is stale.
  W03–W05 have substantial implemented subsets; this report separates those from
  the still-missing user flows. Old test counts remain dated historical evidence.
- APK-only static evidence can establish a request contract without proving the
  working server response, native callback ordering, codecs or deployment ABI.
  Resolve each remaining field through targeted decode and actual use; do not
  invent constants or treat `/sdk/*` dispatch labels as web endpoints.
- Native binary deployment rights/runtime compatibility and provider/merchant
  configuration are release questions for applicable paths. They are not an
  excuse to suspend unrelated APK-derived development.
- Production identity provider, gateway placement, device/model/firmware and
  account role/entitlement matrix need explicit deployment evidence. The user has
  stated that equipment and logged-in accounts are ready for testing; their
  preparation does not itself establish a completed web path.
- Payments, unlock/alarm, firmware, deletions and other live side effects retain
  the existing scoped authorization/readback requirements. UNKNOWN outcomes must
  not be silently retried or counted as success.
- [The supplied design reference](design/2026-09-30-dashboard-reference.md) is
  available. Remaining screens and final visual acceptance still need it applied;
  development screenshots are not final production design acceptance.

## Task 6 integration rulings (2026-10-06, Asia/Seoul)

Ruling: R155 — Prepare and retain the original recorder custody before native
work. RecorderMasterFactory.prepare_master returns a PreparedRecorderMaster
implementing OriginalMuxBindingIssuer and EpochReceiptIssuer. Register that
exact issuer/custody with SQL admission before start(credentials, transport,
budget). binding_for returns None until the original login establishes the
actual wire epoch. Preparation performs no SDK/login or unowned OS work.
Short SQL prepare_viewer/prepare_dedicated selection methods return before any
native wait; credentials are decrypted only for the dedicated path or creator.
Why: the draft open_master return occurred too late for original registration,
and legacy open retains a transaction across a native callback. Cost if wrong:
provider/admission port rework; no relaxed ownership or fabricated wire identity.

Ruling: R156 — Worker focus reservation redeems a purpose-bound ticket;
reserve_focus(ticket, budget) delegates to admission.redeem_focus. Session,
direction and selector belong to the app-role ticket issuer in Task 9. Public
wall stop also redeems a stop-purpose ticket before using its retained wall
scope. A wall UUID alone is insufficient worker authority. Why: app-role issue
cannot be performed by the worker or reconstructed from an opaque lease.
Cost if wrong: RPC/coordinator contract rework, never wider worker authority.

Ruling: R157 — Dedicated main remains a distinct exclusive lease/session path.
prepare_dedicated returns PreparedDedicatedMain with exact native and viewer
receipt issuers before operations; register_dedicated precedes start. Shared
executor read_dedicated_frames serves main stream 1 and read_mux_frames serves
validated substream 2. Legacy read_frames rejects in shared mode. Why: mux slot
identity/validated frames deliberately restrict stream 2, and original cleanup
must be registered before starting main. Cost if wrong: separate media/provider
adapter rework; no main frame relabelled as a mux substream.

Ruling: R158 — Prepare and retain each viewer before cut/decode/peer work.
PreparedMuxViewer implements MuxViewerHandle and ViewerReceiptIssuer; exact
register_viewer_custodian precedes start. Partial/uncertain resources stay
retained. replace_grant takes the exact retained old object and a fresh grant
of the same immutable purpose/scope and swaps references atomically while
denying obsolete publications. Why: cleanup issuers must predate operations,
and immutable refresh must not leave consumers using obsolete authority.
Cost if wrong: provider/media contract rework and retained pending resources.

Task 6 remains active; Tasks 1–5 retain their completed source gates. The narrow
Task 5 selection/control facade refinement is delegated to its original author
in disjoint files while Task 6 works on coordinator ports and inert tests.
No stage2 SDK, decoder, recorder or Chrome acceptance has run.


Ruling: R159 ? Add worker-only0016 viewer_selection(AUTH), returning only
channel selection under locking current wall/actor/worker/channel/source/secret
predicates while leaving the lease LOGIN. Do not invoke legacy media_use for
shared sub selection or broaden viewer_admit to CLAIMED. Selection runs before
master_reserve, so no already-created master is required. Dedicated main still
uses media_use exactly once after its original custody registration. Why:
media_use transitions LOGIN to CLAIMED, preventing the required viewer_admit
operation and unnecessarily exposes encrypted credential columns. Cost if
wrong: additive SQL/facade rework;0013?15 remain untouched. The facade's wall
stop redemption checks the actual embedded STOP purpose before any coordinator
mutation; a heartbeat ticket cannot acquire stop authority.
The original SQL author owns this narrow refinement, with watched causal SQL
RED, scoped GREEN and fresh review required. Task6 remains active; no new
recorder/native/Chrome execution is implied.


Ruling: R160 ? Credential-free viewer_selection/prepare_viewer supports both
valid stream selectors for executor dispatch, superseding only R159's sub-only
restriction. Main additionally requires its exact current FORWARD pending
focus, original master and matching worker/channel/wall/source/secret under
locking LIVE. No decryption or LOGIN transition occurs. It does not issue mux
substream authority. Dedicated preparation still registers separate exact
native and viewer issuers before once-only legacy media_use and starts outside
SQL. Why: an opaque lease exposes no stream, and the executor must select the
appropriate distinct path before operations. Cost if wrong: selector/focus
predicate rework; main is never labelled as a mux slot/frame.


Ruling: R161 ? Add explicit WALL_HEARTBEAT control purpose. App-role issue
performs wall_read(session,wall,true) in the same transaction; a denied renewal
issues no ticket. The one-use ticket expires within20s and renewed wall expiry.
A separate worker wall_heartbeat_redeem requires that exact purpose, current
wall and every retained master/slot, and writes no TTL. Existing STOP redemption
remains unchanged and wrong-purpose attempts do not consume either ticket.
Coordinator refreshes and atomically swaps each registered transport/slot/viewer
grant before heartbeat success. Why: the required heartbeat RPC had no ticket
implementation, despite app SQL renewal existing. Cost if wrong: control/facade
rework; expired grants and pending focus deadlines are never revived.

Ruling: R162 ? Add app-only bounded focus_read for exact scoped public identity,
state and expiry, with no authority token. Keep existing focus_settle SQL void;
Task9 uses scoped readback rather than inferring success from a no-op. Worker
redeem_focus_settle returns only the redeemed wall/ref UUIDs and CANCELLED or
FAILED transition, and coordinator matches its exact retained reference.
Client ATTACHED/native cleanup remains forbidden. Why: void settlement cannot
establish a public success state, and worker callbacks require purpose-bound
control. Cost if wrong: readback contract rework; no wider public authority.

These are narrow intertask SQL/coordinator refinements. Watched causal SQL
RED/GREEN and fresh review are required before the ports unlock Tasks7?9.
No stage2 actual native/recorder/Chrome evidence is claimed.


Ruling: R163 ? Expose focus_identity(grant) as (focus_id,wall_id) from the exact
private issued-grant registry; copied/foreign grants deny. Expired lookup is
permitted only for cleanup and grants no authority. Standardize the settlement
control result as (focus_id,wall_id,CANCELLED|FAILED). Why: FocusGrant carries
its source lease but no focus_id, so control tickets cannot otherwise map to
an exact retained reference. Cost if wrong: accessor/order rework, no authority
reconstruction or deadline extension.

Ruling: R164 ? PreparedMuxViewer.release_frames(exact_tuple) and executor
release_mux_frames(lease_id,exact_tuple) return the original Task3 credits only
after downstream decode/discard/cancel ownership settles. Cleanup release is
allowed while STOPPING; reads/publication are not. The original viewer remains
retained until its exact receipt. Why: parent reads retain encoded credit and
the draft viewer interface lacked a return path, causing permanent charges or
premature refund. Cost if wrong: credit-adapter rework; never free a held batch
or mint a replacement credit as proof of original release.
